# TODO — Open Tasks

> Active/backlog tasks only. Completed work lives in `todo_closed.md` (archive). The `/todo` skill reads THIS file for task selection and moves finished tasks to `todo_closed.md`.
>
> **Task shape** (governed by the task-taxonomy spec): every task is a top-level `- [ ]` line carrying exactly one `[type:bug|feature|design|maint]` tag (first bracket), a plain-English summary, a `[P1|P2|P3]` marker, and an optional `[subject:*]` area tag — with a nested `- Details:` bullet holding file refs, metadata, edge cases, and lineage. Subsystem `##`/`###` headers group by *area*, never by type.

Today the only mechanism is the hard-coded class/instance flag `PAPER_EXCLUDED`, read by `apply_paper_overrides` (`paper_display.py` ~:1204): cardinal `SectionMarker`s (the four elevation markers, `section_marker.py` ~:273) never plot, placed section markers always plot, and gridline bubbles, level datums and blocks have no switch. - [ ] [type:bug] A model-plan text inside a sheet viewport crashes the sheet render and PDF export [P2] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: found by LT1 Task 8 (2026-10-04), PROVEN PRE-EXISTING at `7e1dfe3` and `864aa2a` (VC7). `TextItem.data` is a `@property` (text_item.py ~288, since C5 `63d82e6`) that shadows `QGraphicsItem.data()`, so `apply_paper_overrides`' origin check `item.data(0)` raises `TypeError: 'TextAnnotationData' object is not callable`; inside a viewport paint this native-aborts (exit 127 in `export_pdf`). Fix: call `QGraphicsItem.data(item, 0)` there and audit every `.data(` call on scene items in the paper / isolation paths; then add the full-export text-border Thin Lines parity guard LT1 could only write at paper-pass level (`tests/test_lt1_thin_lines.py`). `paper_display.py`, `text_item.py`. ref: linetypes.md LT1-8.
  - **Partly fixed by SV2 (2026-10-09, `df9b0938`):** the `apply_paper_overrides` origin check now uses `QGraphicsItem.data(item, 0)` (guard `tests/test_sv2_paper_primitives.py::test_text_in_viewport_scene_renders`); the SV2 review found the remaining `paper_display.py` `.data(` calls touch underlay children only. Still open: audit the level-isolation path (`SheetViewport._begin_isolation` / `LevelManager.apply_to_scene`) for `.data(` on scene items, a real-MainWindow repro with a model-plan text in a plan viewport + PDF export, and the LT1 Thin Lines parity guard below.
  - MW seam round (2026-10-07): also why the frozen paper mapping (`paper_legacy_px`) was not applied to `TextItem._frame_pen` -- a model TextItem still can't reach `apply_paper_overrides` (TypeError at `item.data(0)`); re-check text-frame paper width when this is fixed.

## Text annotation system

> Feature landed 2026-09-22 on `feat/text-annotation-frame` (frame axis + FontSelect + fill model + grouped annotation property panel + custom Selector/Stepper/Swatch inputs + tokenized panel metrics `theme.M.PROP_*`). Governing spec `docs/specs/text-annotation-system.md`. Follow-ups below.

- [ ] [type:feature] Text style presets + per-property overrides + SHX (draft-spec phase 2) [P2] [subject:UX]
  - Details: the deferred larger vision from `Downloads/Ribbon Text Group — Spec.md` (seed): named `TextStyle` bundles (font/height/width-factor/B-I-U) + per-entity overrides + the launcher/style-manager dialog + SHX fonts (FontSelect already has a font-source seam). ref: text-annotation-system D1/D2.
- [ ] [type:feature] Extend the ribbon Text/Frame groups to model/Block-Editor text (undo-routed) [P2] [subject:UX]
  - Details: the ribbon Text/Frame groups are paper-scoped (`_font_group_targets` returns targets only on a `PaperSpaceWidget`; only `paper_scene.selectionChanged` drives `_update_font_group_context`). Wire model/Block-Editor selection + route model-text commits through the scene undo snapshot. Overlaps the "Absorb Modify→Text into the entity-aware Font group" item. ref: text-annotation-system D6. `main.py`.
- [ ] [type:feature] Paper text inline edit parity with the model primitive [P2] [subject:UX]
  - Details: 2026-09-22 grill decision — the model-surface primitive sets the edit contract (spec text-annotation-system § "Inline edit (model surface) — AS-BUILT": commit-always exits, editor owns every key but Ctrl+S, single undo step, empty-deletes, text-wins-over-centre-grip, self-painted caret). Paper text keeps its older behaviour (dashed #88aaff edit frame, `_on_edit_finished`, `commit_place_text`, paper QUndoStack). Bring paper onto the same contract (likely a paper-side `TextEditController` sharing the predicate + funnel). `firepro3d/paper_space.py`, `firepro3d/text_item.py`.
- [ ] [type:bug] A real Content change in the property panel mid-inline-edit wipes the live typing and double-pushes undo [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: seam review 2026-09-23 (M6). The stale focus-out replay (`value == _data.text`) is now a no-op, but a GENUINE Content edit in the panel while the same box is inline-editing calls `setPlainText` (discards typed text) and `set_property` pushes a step, then the session commit pushes a second. Decide: either end the inline session before applying a panel Content change, or disable the Content row while inline-editing. `firepro3d/text_item.py` `_set_property_model`, `firepro3d/property_manager.py`.
- [ ] [type:design] Ribbon Font-group combos end a live model inline edit (the panel doesn't) [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: seam review 2026-09-23 (m5). The Font group's editable family/size combos take focus on click and are not the `PropertyManager`, so the inline-edit focus policy commits. Moot while the ribbon is paper-scoped (D6); decide when "Extend the ribbon Text/Frame groups to model text" lands (keep-live list vs commit). `firepro3d/text_item.py` `_focus_out_keeps_edit`, `firepro3d/font_group.py`.
- [ ] [type:bug] `TextItem` scene-mm font sizing rounds to an integer pixel size — small annotative text gets whole-pixel glyph advances and a short cap (3 mm → 4 px, cap 2.86 mm) [P2] [subject:UX]
  - Details: SB3 U1 smoke 2026-10-28 (user: "letter spacing is weird, not sure it is Arial" on the 3 mm RoomTag in the Block Editor and on the canvas). `text_item.py` `_apply_data` scene-mm branch does `f.setPixelSize(round(TEXT_METRIC_REF_PX * h / cap))` so a 3 mm cap becomes a 4 px font: Qt rounds every advance to whole pixels (probe: 'o' 2.0 vs ideal 2.22 px, 'f' 1.0 vs 1.11; `PreferNoHinting` restores fractional advances) and the cap lands at 2.86 mm (= G0's `pb_cap_mm=2.862`). Both the editor `TextItem` and `block_attributes.bound_text_ops` (canvas tags, via `render_outline_path`) share the line. Fix: size the scene-mm font at `TEXT_METRIC_REF_PX` and `setScale(h / cap)` like the paper branch (exact cap, fractional advances); re-baseline the AC10 parity goldens (`tests/test_sb3_parity.py`) and the AC4 cap measurement (`tests/test_sb3_paper.py`, SB3 Task 13) in the same task; check `document().setDocumentMargin` / `setTextWidth` divisors already take `scale`. Pre-existing before SB3 (invisible at the 150 mm model label = 209 px); run right after SB3 merges.
- [ ] [type:maint] Diagnose why the QGraphicsTextItem document renderer hits an engine-less device on the live model viewport [P3] [subject:Architecture]
  - Details: the model surface now sidesteps it entirely (glyph outlines #62 + self-painted caret/selection, 2026-09-23), so this is diagnostic only — relevant if a model-surface text item ever needs Qt's own document rendering. Memory `project_qpainter_engineless_qgraphicstextitem`: never band-aid via `QWidget.paintEngine()`. Needs a live readout at the paint device.

## UI follow-ups

> From the 2026-09-05 UI-cleanup batch.

- [ ] [type:bug] Footer mode badge goes stale on a tab switch [P4] [subject:UX]
  - Details: gridline-hosted blocks smoke-fix review 2026-10-22: `MainWindow._on_tab_changed` (main.py ~1154) never re-syncs the footer badge / instruction to the newly current scene's mode. Since 1a7ee1f3, leaving an armed section placement emits "select" while the new tab is current, so plan left in pipe mode (the tab change resets plan only when `get_placement_anchor()` / `is_input_mode()`) reads SELECT; before, the badge stayed on the previous tab's mode. Same for Paper / 3D tabs. Fix: at the end of `_on_tab_changed` call `self._update_mode_label(<current scene>.mode)` (and a silent section cancel via `SectionScene._drop_place_state` if the emit then becomes redundant); guard: plan in pipe mode -> section tab arm -> back to plan -> badge reads the plan mode.

- [ ] [type:bug] Placement crosshair missing on the visible plan view [P2] [subject:UX]
  - Details: user, 2026-09-08 U3 smoke — confirmed PRE-EXISTING, NOT U3: the U3 branch's only `model_view.py` change is the grip-loop `continue`, which doesn't run during placement; the crosshair enable/draw path is untouched. The accent crosshair (`ui/crosshair`, default ON) is applied once at startup by `main.py:747 _apply_crosshair` looping `self.scene.views()` → `set_crosshair_enabled`. Leading hypothesis: the `Model_Space vestigial-view` bug (`scene.views()` = a hidden index-0 view + one per plan tab, `project_model_space_vestigial_view_bug`) — at startup the crosshair may be enabled only on the vestigial/not-yet-visible view, so the real visible tab never gets it → no crosshair on placement. Diagnostic: toggling Preferences→UI crosshair off/on re-runs `_apply_crosshair` over the current views and (if this is it) restores it. Fix direction: apply the crosshair to the active/visible view (and on tab-open), not just once at startup over `views()[0]`; or gate on `_active_view()`. Live-only. `main.py` (`_apply_crosshair`), `firepro3d/model_view.py` (`_crosshair_enabled`).
- [ ] [type:bug] Title Block editor tab pages paint BLACK live (Overview/Fields/Arrangements) [P2] [subject:UX]
  - Details: user smoke, 2026-09-15 — the `TopTabs` page content area renders flat black in the running app on all three component tabs (Overview info rail, Fields form/info rail, Arrangements). **Live-only: NOT reproducible offscreen** — `dlg.grab()`/`QGraphicsView.grab` under offscreen QPA render the intended `surface` fill correctly (see the many green renders this session), so headless tests can't catch it (`project_live_render_bugs_dodge_headless`, `project_offscreen_qpa_no_fonts_72dpi`). Went ~4 non-converging rounds live-patching; STOPPED per the /todo cascade rule and filed here. **Tried, all failed to fix live:** (1) `content` `QFrame#tbEditorContent { background: surface }` targeted stylesheet; (2) `TopTabs` container + `topTabsBarRow`/`topTabsStackRow`/`topTabsStack` + each page pinned `background: transparent` via targeted objectName selectors; (3) same but pinned OPAQUE `background: <surface>` (per `project_unstyled_qwidget_black_live` "set a bg"). None cleared the black live. **LEADING HYPOTHESIS (try FIRST next session, high-confidence, cheap):** a Qt-stylesheet `background:` rule does NOT paint on a *plain* `QWidget` unless `WA_StyledBackground` is set — the `QStackedWidget` pages (`overview_widget`, `fields_widget`, `ArrangementsTab`) and the `TopTabs` rows are plain `QWidget`s, so their objectName `background:` rule is silently ignored and Qt paints the default (black under the dark house palette). Fix candidates in order: (a) `page.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_StyledBackground, True)` on each page + row in `TopTabs.add_tab`/`__init__` (keeps the stylesheet approach); (b) `page.setAutoFillBackground(True)` + set the page's `QPalette.Window` role to `surface` (palette route — but house-dialog QSS may override palette, `project_unstyled_qwidget_black_live`); (c) give the pages a real styled parent (wrap each page body in a `QFrame` with `WA_StyledBackground`). **Diagnostic protocol:** run the app (FULLY restart — a file edit does NOT reload the process), add a temporary live readout at the black widget (dump `widget.testAttribute(WA_StyledBackground)`, `widget.palette().color(QPalette.Window).name()`, `widget.styleSheet()`, `widget.autoFillBackground()`), trust the running app over the code reading. Separately confirm whether the `QGraphicsView` preview/canvas backdrops (dark area beyond the paper) are part of the same complaint or a distinct "set `setBackgroundBrush(surface)`" item. Solid core (convention rollout, rail dividers, margins, maximize, Drawing-Area→Overview merge) is LANDED + test-green (`3144ff8`, `bb183b0`, `0978230`, `c851209`, `866afb7`, `04ff40b`) on `feat/titleblock-ansi-d-default`. `firepro3d/ui_kit.py` (`TopTabs.__init__`/`add_tab`), `firepro3d/titleblock_editor.py`, `firepro3d/titleblock_arrange.py`. ref: ui-design-system.
## UI Design-System follow-ups

- [ ] [type:maint] `ui_kit.SortHeader` accent caret: draw without `QPainter.drawPolygon` to survive a broken paint engine [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: Level Manager debug 2026-10-30 (`debug_sortheader.md`, session 9b0afee2 scratchpad): during an Explorer shell crash/restart window (08:53-08:59) test runs died with a stack overflow in `SortHeader.paintSection` -> `drawPolygon(QPolygon)`. The crash dump shows a sip-wrapped QPaintEngine that implements neither `drawPolygon` overload, so Qt's two default versions call each other. Not reproducible since the shell came back, and the repo has no `QPaintEngine` subclass. Optional hardening: `fillPath(QPainterPath)`, but prove first that it doesn't hit the same recursion; keep `test_sort_header_paints_an_accent_caret_only_on_the_sort_section` green. `ui_kit.py`.

> From `docs/specs/ui-design-system.md` deferred waves + P4/P5 review notes (2026-09-06).

- [ ] [type:bug] Return in any `HouseDialog` rejects instead of pressing the primary button [P2] [subject:UX]
  - Details: found 2026-09-22 building the colour picker (todo #70); reproduced on an unmodified `HouseDialog` (focus lands on `_WinDot`, Return → result 0). Two causes: (1) `house_dialog.set_footer_buttons` calls `btn.setDefault(True)` while the footer QFrame is unparented, so QDialog never registers it (a repeat `setDefault(True)` is a no-op); (2) the frameless title-bar dots (`frameless_shell._WinDot`, QPushButton) are autoDefault and take initial focus. Fix: parent/attach the footer before `setDefault`, `_WinDot.setAutoDefault(False)` (+ `NoFocus`). Then delete the local workaround in `colour_picker.ColourPickerDialog.__init__`. Guard: a plain HouseDialog + QTest Return → Accepted. `house_dialog.py`, `frameless_shell.py`. ref: ui-design-system D6.
- [ ] [type:maint] Migrate the hand-rolled QPushButton colour swatches onto `ui_kit.Swatch` [P3] [subject:Code Quality]

Five stylesheet-swatch helpers do the same job: `display_manager._update_swatch` (+ section-pattern variants), `titleblock_editor._make_swatch/_update_swatch`, `roof_dialog._update_color_swatch`, inline in `fs_visibility_dialog._pick`. Each migration changes that dialog's layout (Swatch adds a hex label; Display Manager rows are tight + carry hatch previews) → per-dialog parity smoke. All already route through `colour_picker.pick_colour`. User 2026-09-22 (smoke): the **Display Manager** colour chips should match the property-panel `Swatch` style. Do this as part of a full Display Manager chrome revision, not piecemeal.

Checklist in the spec: `PreferencesDialog`, `DisplayManager`, `TitleBlockEditorDialog`, `AutoPopulateDialog`, `SprinklerManagerDialog`, `RoofDialog`, `WallDialog`, `PaperExportDialog`, `ArrayDialog`, `CalibrateDialog`, `ViewRangeDialog`, `ThermalRadiationDialog`, `DesignPointDialog`, `FSVisibilityDialog`, `SectionPatternDialog`, `SheetViewPropertiesDialog`, `RevisionsDialog`, `_RecordEditDialog`, `AlgorithmParamsDialog`. - [ ] [type:maint] Complex-dialog body-container transparency check [P3] [subject:UX]

Verify those containers don't show a dark mismatch in any uncovered gaps (mostly tiled, so likely fine); if they do, set the container transparent or route through a transparent wrapper. `underlay_manager.py`, `block_manager.py`, `underlay_import_dialog.py`.

  - Details: dead `findChild(QDialogButtonBox)` fallback in `underlay_import_dialog._set_controls_enabled`; `apply_stylesheet=False` now leaves the `houseDialog` marker set while blanking the sheet (harmless — marker with no sheet does nothing); pre-existing dead `numericInputRequested` signal (`main._on_numeric_input_requested` is never emitted). `underlay_import_dialog.py`, `underlay_manager.py`, `block_manager.py`, `main.py`.

- [ ] [type:bug] Numeric `QDoubleValidator`s follow the system locale but values parse with `float()` [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: found by the UP1 T5 review (2026-10-19). No `QLocale` is set anywhere in the app, so on a comma-decimal system locale a validator accepts "2,5" (then `float()` fails) and rejects "2.5". UP1 fixed the colour picker's pattern-scale field (`QLocale.c()`); the rest remain: `property_manager.py` (two numeric row validators), `themed_message.themed_input_number`. Fix: one shared C-locale validator factory (or `QLocale.setDefault(QLocale.c())` at startup — decide) + a comma-locale guard test. ref: ui-design-system.

- [ ] [type:maint] Tokenize Title Block canvas/preview painter colours [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: 2026-09-15 tokenization audit (title-block dialog convention rollout). The dialog's *stylesheet* chrome is fully tokenized (TopTabs/SwitchBar/Section/rail-header/`_vrule`/`_pool_rail_qss` all use `detect().<token>`; the only stylesheet hex are the intentional `#000`/`#fff` field-default-ink + white-paper preview, theme-exempt). Remaining findings are **QPainter/backdrop colours** (not caught by the `setStyleSheet` hexguard) that should move to tokens: (1) `StripCanvas.__init__` `setBackgroundBrush(QColor("#505050"))` — canvas backdrop grey → a themed backdrop token (or `surface`); (2) StripCanvas cell-selection outline `QColor("#2f80ed")` (blue, several call-sites) — decide: intentionally distinct-from-accent, or should be `accent` (green)?; (3) `QColor("#555555")` painter pen; (4) the editor preview `QGraphicsView`s (`_preview_view`, `_field_preview_view`) set **no** `setBackgroundBrush`, so they inherit a dark/near-black default backdrop (the field preview's black-below-the-logo) — set an explicit themed brush. All pre-existing except (4)'s visibility surfaced this session. Bundle with the live-black-bg follow-up if that turns out to share a root. `firepro3d/titleblock_arrange.py` (`StripCanvas`), `firepro3d/titleblock_editor.py` (preview views). ref: ui-design-system §D2.

## Block System

> 2D symbol definitions + instances; sibling to Features. Governing spec `docs/specs/block-system.md`; landed slices S1–S4.6 in todo_closed.md.

- [ ] [type:feature] Block placement template in the Properties panel (plan + section) [P3] [subject:Blocks]
  - Details: user smoke 2026-10-22 (gridline-hosted blocks): expected the Properties panel to pre-populate with the block's template while Place Block is armed, as sprinkler / pipe / opening placement do (`current_*_template`, main.py ~221-223). Today plan `place_block` clears the panel (`MainWindow._on_mode_changed_template` -> `show_properties(None)`, main.py ~2598) and the section path matches that (ratified parity, FP1 2026-10-22). Grill first: which rows the template shows (scale, rotation, mirror, level / hosted Reference-Offset-Elevation in a section) and how edits apply to the next placed instance. `firepro3d/property_manager.py`, `main.py`, `firepro3d/section_scene.py`. ref: block-system spec (via SPEC-INDEX).

- [ ] [type:bug] Cut never emits `blockInstancesChanged` -- Blocks/Model browser can stay stale after Cutting a block or a host gridline [P3] [subject:Blocks]
  - Details: found by the gridline-hosted blocks T5 review (2026-10-22). The Cut commit (`modify_tools_controller.py` `press_copy_base`, which calls `Model_Space._bulk_delete`) blocks signals and re-emits only `selectionChanged`; `delete_items` re-emits `blockInstancesChanged`, Cut does not. Pre-existing for plain BlockInstances; since T5 a Cut host gridline also removes its hosted blocks. Repro first: Cut a block, check whether the Model browser "Blocks" group drops the row. Fix: emit `blockInstancesChanged` when `_bulk_delete` removed any block (use its returned count).

- [ ] [type:bug] `Model_Space.open_edit_attributes` parents the Edit Attributes dialog to `views()[0].window()` -- the hidden startup `Model_View` in the real MainWindow [P4] [subject:Blocks]
  - Details: found by the SB3 G4 quality review (2026-10-28). `model_space.py` `open_edit_attributes` picks `self.views()[0].window()` as the dialog parent; in the real app `views()[0]` is the hidden startup `Model_View` (main.py ~227) whose `window()` is itself, so the dialog is parented to a hidden widget (centring / stacking / theme inheritance follow that widget, not the MainWindow). Same mechanism as the `_show_status` bug fixed in 0ef657f1 (SB3), which now iterates `views()` for the first window with a `statusBar`. Repro first through the real window (`mw` fixture): open Edit Attributes on a placed block and assert `dlg.parent() is mw`. Fix: one shared `Model_Space.main_window()` helper (first view whose `window()` is the MainWindow) used by both call sites; grep `views()[0]` for any other parent lookup.

### Schematics build (what = `docs/specs/schematics.md` D-S1–D-S18; how = `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-schematics-concept-design.md` SD1–SD11)

> Filed 2026-10-07 by the schematics concept run (grill Q1–Q18 + brainstorm groups A–C, all ratified). SV1 → SV2 → SV3; SV4 after SV2 (∥ SV3). A schematic = `BlockDefinition` with `kind == "schematic"`, never instanced, authored in the Block Editor, placed on sheets only as a viewport; sheets hold viewports + title block + sheet text and nothing else (D-S2 — supersedes the P1 "Paper-space block placement" task, moved to todo_closed). Guards G1–G6 + keep-green list in the concept doc. Revisit triggers (D-S1): data-driven schematics after SB5/SB6; sheet furniture (north arrow / scale bar) at SB4f; legends = the placed-table feature, not a schematic.

- [ ] [type:bug] Perf guards fail at `main` on this machine: offset drag ~2x the 8 ms bar, D18 solver bars, 10 `-m perf` failures in all [P3] [subject:Testing]
  - Details: found by the SV3 VC6 run (2026-10-09). `tests/test_modify_tools_offset.py::test_offset_real_mouse_move_is_fast` (4 params; drag 15-16 ms vs the 8 ms bar, median 78 ms vs 30 ms) and `tests/test_sketch_solver_perf.py::test_d18_bars` fail identically at the branch base `56753e90` in a separate worktree (VC7) — SV3 touches neither offset nor the solver. The SV2 record (same day) reported no perf failures, so decide machine state (power plan / thermal / background load) vs drift before touching any bar: re-run `-m perf` on a quiet machine first; the full list is in the SV3 closed note. **2026-10-09 SV4 VC6 (same machine, later the same day, TMP/HOME on `D:/Scratch/fp3d_tmp`):** `-m perf` 35 passed, 1 skipped, 1 xfailed, 0 failed at `fa05618d` — none of the SV3 failures reproduced; machine state, not drift, is the leading explanation. +1 occurrence 2026-10-12 (block-origin snap keep-green): `test_offset_real_mouse_move_is_fast` 3 params failed in a 40-file chunk on a 2.4 GB-free host with a concurrent reviewer run, then passed 4/4 alone twice (and `test_hatch_perf` G12 likewise) — load, not drift. +1 occurrence 2026-10-15 (CS7/CS8 VC6, 2.7 GB free): `test_constraint_live_drag.py` rect-heavy grip-drag / diagnostics / commit bars failed 3 in the `[c]` chunk; two interleaved base `31ecf5b1` / HEAD worktree pairs then failed 1 of the 3 on each side alike.
- [ ] [type:bug] Shift+F / Shift+I / Shift+S don't enter flip / mirror / scale in a Block Editor scene [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: `tests/test_modify_tools_shortcuts.py::test_flip_mirror_keys_drive_the_block_editor_scene` (3 params) fails `editor.mode == tool` (mode stays None). Pre-existing: fails identically at `3437a2de` (main + docs) in a separate worktree (VC7, SV2 VC6 run 2026-10-09). Repro the keypress path in a live editor tab first; the test may be the stale party. +1 occurrence 2026-10-12 (block-origin snap VC6): fails 3/3 alone; proved pre-existing at base `c2319eb9` (alternating base/HEAD worktree runs). +1 occurrence 2026-10-15 (CS7/CS8 VC6): 3/3 at base `31ecf5b1` and HEAD in worktrees. +1 occurrence 2026-10-15 (CS9 VC6): 3/3 in chunk 05; the file fails 3/29 at base `d5f4813b` and HEAD `4b90c010` in one worktree. +1 occurrence 2026-10-17 (AC2 keep-green): 3 failed / 26 passed at base `33660ce2` and HEAD in alternating worktree runs. Duplicates of this line sit in the "Code Review Audit" and later testing sections -- fold them into this one. +1 occurrence 2026-10-17 (AC4 VC6): 3 failed in the m-r chunk; alone 2 failed / 1 passed at base `3db589c7` and HEAD alike. +1 occurrence 2026-10-22 (gridline-hosted blocks T4 keep-green): 3 failed / 42 passed at base `d8d3d987` (worktree) and HEAD `621bf9a8`. +1 occurrence 2026-10-22 (gridline-hosted blocks T8-9 fix keep-green): 3 failed / 26 passed at branch base `6c122632` (worktree) and HEAD `8e00d1b3` alike.
  - +1 occurrence 2026-10-23 (wall polish T5 keep-green): same 3 fail at branch base `c9a5bddd` and HEAD (VC7, worktrees).
  - +1 occurrence 2026-10-26 (editor-build S2 T1 keep-green): Shift+I / Shift+S fail (Shift+F passes alone) at branch base `d2ac14b0` and HEAD `5466f100` in one worktree (VC7).
  - +1 occurrence 2026-10-27 (editor-build S3 T5 keep-green): 3 failed / 26 passed alone at branch base `88c146c1` and HEAD `5254dcd8` in one worktree (VC7).
- [ ] [type:bug] Dropping a view at a true scale can overflow the sheet — the drop clamp is discarded [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: `PaperGraphicsView.dropEvent` clamps vp_w/vp_h to the sheet, but for scale > 0 `SheetViewport.__init__` → `_reconnect_source` → `_recompute_size_from_scale` overwrites them with extent × scale, so the box can spill off the sheet (x/y were computed for the clamped size). Plans and schematics alike; a scaled schematic has no resize handles (D-S10), so only a scale change fixes it. Found by the SV2 group C + seam reviews 2026-10-09. Options: warn, fall back to NTS, or pick the largest preset that fits.
- [ ] [type:bug] A scaled detail viewport keeps a stale box after paper undo / redo across a marker crop change [P4] [subject:UX]
  - Details: `PaperScene._resync_viewport` re-derives the box from the live extent only for schematics (SV2 `30d712e0`); details also crop live, so a detail crop edited between a viewport command's push and its undo restores the old w/h. `tests/test_paper_commands.py::test_viewport_geometry_command_redo_undo` pins verbatim w/h restore — decide the contract for live-crop kinds before widening the fix.
- [ ] [type:maint] SV2 seam-review minors [P4] [subject:Code Quality]
  - Details: (a) `scene.load_from_file` emits `blockDefinitionsChanged` before `MainWindow._apply_loaded_file` calls `schematic_scenes.dispose_all()`, so `_refresh_schematic_viewports` builds render scenes for the old sheet that are thrown away (harmless waste) — skip the refresh while loading; (b) the `"schematic"` view-type string is bare in 6 modules (as are plan / detail / elevation) — consider view-type constants in `constants.py` for all kinds; (c) `tests/test_sv2_mainwindow.py::test_g3_edit_and_save_refits_every_viewport` asserts the private `_effective_crop()` — add a pixel-extent check.
- [ ] [type:maint] Doc drift found at the SV1 Account (pre-existing) [P4] [subject:Documentation]
  - Details: SV1 Account review 2026-10-08, none introduced by SV1. (1) `ribbon-bar.md` is `status: current` at `2a22ba9` but predates the LT4 / LT5 / WM2 Block Editor button changes -- its button roster never names Pattern Tile / Linetype / End Type; re-audit the Block Editor tab section. (2) `project-browser.md` Goal / Motivation / Acceptance Criteria were not re-read at the SV1 Account (stamped `f1d8151` / `91a1d38`) -- check for stale "Schematics stub" wording; the `activateModelSpace` row says "any `ms_stub`" while folder roles now exclude `schematic_root` / `schematic_series`. (3) `schematics.md` D-S14 reads as if the "Also save as Template" toggle exists (it lands in SV3); D-S16's verb list vs the Acceptance line's "six verbs" counts New ambiguously; D-S4 doesn't name the Save Block library tree that SV1 also filters. Fix wording only.
- [ ] [type:bug] Undoing a block's or schematic's first Save leaves its editor tab bound to the removed id -- the next Save silently does nothing [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: SV1 seam review #5 (2026-10-08). The tab keeps `_edit_block_id` of a definition the undo removed; `save()` takes the edit path and `commit_block_definition` returns None with no message (`block_editor.py` `save` / `_saved_definition`, `model_space.py` edit branch). Plain blocks behave the same; schematics make it easy to hit (G1's "Ctrl+Z after Save" flow removes the leaf while the tab stays). Expected: Save falls back to the Save dialog (a new definition) or the tab re-binds on redo. ref: block-system.md Block Editor contract, schematics.md D-S17.
- [ ] [type:bug] File → Open / Open Recent discard an unsaved project without the save prompt [P2] [subject:UX]
  - Details: found at the SV1 fix round (2026-10-08), pre-existing. `main.py` `open_file` / `_open_recent` never call `_ask_save_changes` (only `new_file` and `closeEvent` do), so opening a project while the current one is modified loses its changes silently. Expected: the same Save / Discard / Cancel prompt New uses, after the SV1 unsaved-schematics prompt.
- [ ] [type:bug] A failed project open still runs the post-load restore and sweeps the still-loaded project's view and Schematic tabs [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: SV1 seam review (2026-10-08), pre-existing. `scene_io.load_from_file` returns early on an unreadable file, but `_load_project` runs `_apply_loaded_file` anyway (levels, browser, `_close_stale_view_tabs`, active plan, resolver rebind) against the unchanged scene; since SV1 that sweep also closes Schematic editor tabs. Expected: a failed load leaves the session untouched (load returns a success flag the caller checks).
- [ ] [type:bug] Closing the app discards dirty Block / Schematic editor tabs without a prompt [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: found at the SV1 fix round (2026-10-08), pre-existing. `closeEvent` asks only about the project (`_ask_save_changes`); unsaved editor work is dropped. Reuse the SV1 `_confirm_discard_dirty_schematics` shape, generalized to every dirty editor tab.

Silent Save keeps name/library/series and Save As makes a NEW id, so the editor can no longer rename a saved block in place (keep `id`, instances follow) — only the Manager's collision Rename reaches `set_block_metadata`. Options: a "Block Properties…" editor verb opening `BlockSaveDialog` in "edit" context (which also restores the "updates N instances" notice), or inline rename in the Blocks browser / Manager. `block_editor.py`, `blocks_browser.py`, `block_manager.py`. ref: `block-system.md` "Save, import placement & library".
  - Widened 2026-09-30 (nested-blocks smoke): also change LIBRARY and SERIES in place — the user asked "how do i switch the metadata in app?"; today `set_block_metadata` is reachable only through the Manager's save-collision Rename (name only).

Above N selected items (~100) show frame + move only, no per-item grips. `selection_manipulator.py` `_active_handles`; ref `selection-manipulator.md`.

- [ ] [type:bug] A pattern copy can rob a Hatch-folder override of its id when the override folder sits inside the block library [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: disk-scan review R5 (2026-10-10). With the Hatch patterns folder set to e.g. `blocks/MyHatches` and a same-filename copy in `blocks/Archive/Old`, both files are inconsistent; the library walk's owner tie goes to walk order (Archive) while `capability_folder.scan` (Hatch folder only) offers the MyHatches file, so `load_blocks_from_files` re-ids it and the referenced pattern stays missing. Fix: let the capability load pass its folder as the owner context, or prefer a file inside the configured capability folder. `block_library.py`, `capability_folder.py`, `hatch_patterns.py`.
- [ ] [type:maint] Two projects saving the same library block can leave a same-id twin on disk [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: disk-scan review N3 (2026-10-10). P1 renames + saves (its source file re-filed); P2's `source_path` then points at a missing file, so P2's Save removes nothing and P1's renamed file stays as a second same-id file (listed as a duplicate). Not data loss. Options: fall back to the consistent-owner rule when the source is gone. `block_library._previous_file`.
- [ ] [type:maint] Block library walk cost: repeated walks per Save / load / ensure, first refresh parses every `.fpdb` [P3] [subject:Performance]
  - Details: disk-scan review M5 (2026-10-10). `save_to_library` walks 2–3× (`find_collision`, `_previous_file`), `ensure_block_loaded` 2× (`is_copy` + the walk in `load_blocks_from_files`), `source_status` once per Manager row; the first walk json-parses every file (was: small index files). Confirm the metric with the user (library size, which interaction) and bench before fixing (memoize one walk per operation; a cheap header read). `block_library.py`, `blocks_browser.py`, `block_manager.py`.
- [ ] [type:maint] Guard gaps on the disk-scan copy paths: canvas drop and Block Editor Open picker [P3] [subject:Testing]
  - Details: disk-scan review M6 (2026-10-10). `model_view._drop_block` and `BlockOpenDialog._accept_choice` now use `ensure_block_loaded`'s resolved id but have no real-path guard placing / opening a copied (duplicate-id) file. Add VC3 guards through a shown view drop and the dialog.

Kind is frozen in-app (D-S3), so the only fix today is a hand edit. Decide: list it greyed with "not a schematic" + a Convert verb, or a footer count of skipped files. `schematic_new_dialog.py`, `schematics.md` D-S15.

  - Details: S2.x smoke follow-up. v1 uses the selection bounding-box top-left as the block origin (modeless, testable); wire a `set_scale`-style transient single-click snapped origin-pick so the user chooses the insertion base. `main.py`, `model_space.py`.
- [ ] [type:maint] HALO hover over one huge block costs ~14 s per mouse move (pre-existing) [P2] [subject:Performance]
  - Details: block-primitive-snap bench (2026-10-15): one authored block of 16k lines + 4k arcs on the plan, select mode, zoom 0.2 -- median 14.2-14.7 s/move at base `b6ae9522` and 14.2-17.7 s at HEAD (interleaved pairs; wall-mode snap hover is ~0.3 s). Unlike the loose-item 'HALO hover on huge drawings' task (scene.items prefilter, ~50 ms), the cost is per-item: `halo_pick_distance_px` walks the block's whole posed trace (`toSubpathPolygons` over every subpath) and Qt tests the IntersectsItemShape candidate box against the whole shape. Profile first, then consider culling the trace to the aperture (the posed stand-ins + bounds from `snap_primitives` already exist). `halo.py`, `block_instance.py`.
- [ ] [type:maint] First hover over a large block builds every posed snap stand-in (+~1.2 s once) [P3] [subject:Performance]
  - Details: block-primitive-snap bench (2026-10-15): first wall-mode hover on a 20k-primitive block 2.6-2.7 s at HEAD vs 1.3-1.8 s at base (user accepted). `BlockInstance.snap_primitives` builds a scene-free item per primitive on first use, per instance and per pose (N instances of one big block build N times); `halo_area_path` unites fills / text boxes one `united` at a time and `shape()` re-strokes + unites per zoom level. Options: build lazily per near-cursor region, share a definition-local set across instances, unite once. Bench per the deferred-repaint rule before fixing.
- [ ] [type:maint] Move handle snap: a block's primitive centres share rank 0 with its insertion point [P4] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: T2-3 review (2026-10-15): `handle_snap` ranks every `center` handle 0, so a block with many circles / arcs fills `HANDLE_SNAP_MAX_HANDLES` with centres and can crowd endpoints out (the insertion point stays first: emit order + stable sort). Only act if a real block shows it -- e.g. rank the insertion point -1 and primitive centres with the rest. `handle_snap.py`, `snap_engine._block_snaps`.

Footer already reads "N of M blocks · K instances" (adequate — left as-is). Remaining/optional: group-count roll-up N/A (flat). `block_manager.py`.

Add a registry-wide invalidate at the end of the project-role `_restore_network` and scene_io load. `model_space.py`, `scene_io.py`, `block_registry.py`.

### System Blocks build (concept: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-29-system-blocks-concept-design.md`)

> Filed 2026-09-29 by the System Blocks concept run (grill Q1–Q16 + architecture F1–F8, all ratified except F6 = proposal pending its probe). Build order = the concept doc's Build Order (phases 0–7); phase 0 = merge `feat/nested-blocks` (the open Nested blocks task). **Reassess checkpoint after SB3** — stop/redesign if the G6 perf guard fails, bound text can't share static ops, or the slice balloons. Each task carries its F8 spec amendments; guards G1–G7 per the concept doc.

- [ ] [type:bug] SB1a follow-up: non-flatten Explode copies only the outer instance's present values -- a child that redeclares a key with a different default shows its own default after Explode [subject:Architecture] [P3]
  - Details: VC9 F3 (2026-10-21): `block_explode.explode_instances` (flatten=False) copies `inst.attributes` onto the child, so a key the outer definition redeclared with a different `default` and the user never typed re-resolves against the child's default (A1 said outermost wins). Copy the outer effective values (`resolve_values` output) for the child's keys instead. `block_explode.py`.
- [ ] [type:bug] SB1a follow-up: one corrupt attribute record aborts a .fpd / .fpdb load (`normalise_attr` ValueError) [subject:Architecture] [P3]
  - Details: VC9 F4 + G1 minors (2026-10-21): `normalise_attr` raises a bare ValueError on `precision` 'abc' / '2.5' and accepts a negative precision (`format_value` then misbehaves); a definition carrying one such record fails the whole project load. Skip the record with a warning (the DXF-import idiom), clamp precision >= 0, and guard both. `block_attributes.py`, `block_definition.py`.
- [ ] [type:bug] SB1a follow-up: bound-text snap stand-ins are sized to the authored `@[key]` token, not the resolved glyphs [subject:Architecture] [P3]
  - Details: VC9 F5 (2026-10-21): `text_snap_points` builds its stand-in from the primitive dict's raw text, so a placed instance's snap targets sit on the token's box while the painted (and hit-tested, F1) glyphs are the resolved string's. Resolve through the instance's values when the snap geometry is built for an instance. `block_definition.py`, `block_snap_geometry.py`, `block_instance.py`.
- [ ] [type:bug] SB1a follow-up: a host-value change re-shapes bound text without `prepareGeometryChange` [subject:Architecture] [P3]
  - Details: G2 review minor + plan note (2026-10-21): `BlockInstance.set_attributes` and the pull-on-paint `host_values()` path change glyph geometry (bounds / shape) without `prepareGeometryChange`, so the BSP index can hold a stale rect; SB2's provider must push a geometry change (or `_bound_ops` must call it on a key miss). Fold into SB2's provider seam. `block_instance.py`.
  - +1 occurrence 2026-10-24 (SB2a T3 review): SB2a wired `refresh_bound()` for the panel commit and units change only. Remaining: (a) undo/redo of a host edit and deleting a host should call `refresh_bound` on `instances_bound_to(item)`; (b) paint-path cost: `BlockInstance.host_values()` rebuilds the `HostValue` dict on every paint, so cache it between refreshes once SB3 sets real hosts. `block_instance.py`, `model_space.py`. ref: element-parameters.md D2.
- [ ] [type:bug] SB1a follow-up: Explode turns non-keep_upright text 180 degrees on a mirrored instance outside (-90, 90] while the bound render leaves it unturned [subject:Architecture] [P4]
  - Details: G3 review m2 (2026-10-21, pre-existing for bound text): `block_explode._pose_text` applies the readable turn to every text primitive, but the bound layer renders a non-keep_upright prim with `readable=False`, so Explode of a mirrored instance at such an angle changes the look. Decide which is right (A5 says keep_upright is the only pose-only rule) and align the two. `block_explode.py`, `block_instance.py`.
- [ ] [type:bug] SB1a follow-up: `TextItem.begin_edit` swaps the raw text in without `prepareGeometryChange` / `_apply_format` [subject:Architecture] [P4]
  - Details: G5 review minor (2026-10-21, filed not applied -- runtime-behaviour change): on a Block Editor text with `@[key]` fields, entering inline edit shows the authored token in place of the sample value by swapping `_display_text` without the geometry/format pass, so bounds and format may flash stale until commit. `text_item.py` (begin_edit).
- [ ] [type:maint] SB1a follow-up: redundant sample-preview passes + dead helper + test/edge minors [subject:Architecture] [P4]
  - Details: VC9 F5 + G5 minors (2026-10-21): `refresh_sample_preview` runs twice on seed / `_restore_state`, per item in `_add_from_dict` (O(n^2) on a large seed) and per item in `apply_attributes_edit`, and unconditionally on `_restore_network` (a no-op off-editor) -- one pass per commit; `block_attributes.rename_key_in_texts` has no production caller (`rename_keys_in_string` is the one used) -- retire or wire; `rename_key_in_texts` silently merges when the new key is already referenced; renaming Y to a just-removed X re-points existing `@[X]` tokens (document or refuse); `tests/test_sb1a_instance.py` flip_threshold guard should also assert `shape()` differs at 91 vs 89; `_assert_fields` 'attributes not in primitives[1]' is a no-op assertion. `block_editor.py`, `model_space.py`, `block_attributes.py`, `tests/test_sb1a_*.py`.
- [ ] [type:bug] Demote / Check out handlers: OSError escapes the Qt slot; verbs offered on a non-shipped System-row copy [subject:Architecture] [P3]
  - Details: 2026-10-28 T3 review minors. (1) An `OSError` from `system_promote.demote` / `checkout` / `promote` (locked or read-only file) propagates out of `MainWindow._demote_block_from_browser` / `_checkout_block_from_browser` / `_promote_block_from_browser` -- catch it beside `PromoteRefused` and show `themed_info` in all three. (2) `BlocksBrowser._build_context_menu` offers Check out / Demote on any System-library leaf, including a project-embedded System copy whose id is not in `block_library.shipped_index()` -- gate the two verbs on `block_id in shipped_index()` (today the click just gets the 'Only a System block' refusal). (3) T1 M2: `system_promote.demote` removes only the immediate emptied series folder; a nested series (`Series/Sub/`) leaves empty parents -- walk up to `system_root()`. `main.py`, `firepro3d/blocks_browser.py`, `firepro3d/system_promote.py`. ref: block-system.md Authoring path.

Tests to add in `tests/test_system_demote.py`: nested series demote; meta name != filename; a mid-demote failure (write ok, delete fails) leaves both files; `block_library.scan_tiered` sees the Workbench copy after demote; unreadable file at the Workbench target -> `overwrites == (stem, 'unreadable')` and replaced; `MainWindow._overwrite_warning` lines + the 'Overwrite' OK label; `MainWindow._warn_missing_patterns` status-bar message on New/Open. - [ ] [type:bug] SB1b follow-up: System-tier robustness minors (review findings, filed not applied) [subject:Architecture] [P3]
  - Details: SB1b reviews 2026-10-23. (1) `block_library._under_system` (~:122) and `system_library._inside` (~:113) duplicate one prefix test on abspath+normcase -> one public helper using `os.path.realpath` both sides (junctions / 8.3 names) (M1 + VC9-m2). (2) `is_reserved_library` misses a trailing-dot 'System.' (Windows strips it) (M3). (3) `save_to_library` (~:388) stamps `base_version` before the write -> restore the old value on OSError, else an edited copy reads 'update available' (T4-M1). (4) `system_library._reid` non-dict JSON raises AttributeError past the per-file `(OSError, ValueError)` guard -> raise ValueError (T5f-M1); a failed `_restamp_library` is never retried -> report it in `skipped` and rescan `Styles/` for leftover 'System' stamps at start (T5f-M2); `block_library._atomic_write_json` leaves `*.tmp` on a failed replace -> unlink (T5f-M3). (5) `blocks_browser` padlock / read-only tooltip keyed on the Library name only (~:456-480) -> decide per leaf via `is_system_block(id)` (T78-M1). (6) `main._duplicate_block_from_browser` load-failure message shows the uuid -> pass the leaf name (T78-M2). (7) `block_editor._save_via_dialog` Save As on a System-origin copy starts on Library 'System' -> blank the initial library; `run_duplicate_dialog` keeps nested System-id deps verbatim in `bundled` (latent) (T78-M4). (8) `block_manager._duplicate_and_retarget` returns silently when the written copy fails to load / its id is not loaded (~:861-868): themed_info 'Saved, but could not load the copy' + `model.refresh()`, and re-open `edit_definition(defn.id)` when the old tab was closed (T10-M1 + VC9f-M1). (9) `hatch_patterns.shipped_patterns_dir` / `shipped_pattern_files` (~:59-97) bypass `system_root()` -> drop the `preview_tile` fallback (~:164-166), keep shipped files for the migration byte-compare only (VC9-m1). (10) `library_tree_for` (block_editor.py ~:56-67) unions embedded System Libraries into the normal Save dialog -> filter `is_reserved_library` there, drop the filter in `run_duplicate_dialog` (VC9-m3). (11) `retarget_block_refs` H7 calls `apply_saved_display_settings` which discards unsaved Display Manager tweaks (same as every undo restore, model_space.py ~:3670) -> re-derive only the touched items' section cache (T9-M2); H3 assigns `fill_layers` directly instead of `item._set_fill_layer("pattern", ...)` (T9-M3); H2 nested-host repaint unguarded. `block_library.py`, `system_library.py`, `blocks_browser.py`, `block_editor.py`, `block_manager.py`, `hatch_patterns.py`, `model_space.py`, `main.py`.
- [ ] [type:maint] SB1b follow-up: guard gaps + test-shape minors [subject:Testing] [P4]
  - Details: SB1b reviews 2026-10-23. Add guards: nested-dep stamp loops on load / reload (`model_space` ~:2315 / ~:2248 `for b in bundled`, RED on that line) (T4-M3); same-name different-id user hatch labelled 'Name (library)' (T6-M2); MainWindow duplicate of an embedded System leaf (path == '') (T78-M3); legacy-alias ref ('diagonal') on an H5 override / H3 item (T9-M1); btn_save enabled with `_SYSTEM_SAVE_TIP` on a modified System copy + a nested bundle through `bundle_for` (T10-M2, T10-M3); I2 Discard branch, dialog-cancel tab restore with a tab open, refile undo, refile collision with a different block, refile `set_block_metadata` False (VC9f-M3). Reshape: `test_g5_system_first_then_user_dedup_by_id` `rows[:5]` -> `rows[:len(_shipped_ids())]` (T6-M1); G2 MainWindow / manager guards stub `run_duplicate_dialog` with hand-copied `_write_copy` bodies (test_system_library_browser.py:107, test_system_library_manager.py:59) -> stub only `BlockSaveDialog.exec` (VC9-m4); `test_g6_update_available_pill_token` asserts the constant -> assert the painted colour vs `theme.color("accent")` (VC9-m5); `test_vc9_i2_manager_save_reopens_the_editor_on_the_copy` covers the clean-close path only -> dirty the tab or rename (VC9f-M4); `blocks_browser.library_only_entries` docstring (~:217) -> mention the `system=False` variant (T11-M2). `tests/test_system_library_*.py`, `tests/test_block_save_library.py`.
- [ ] [type:maint] Property panel calls `get_properties()` per target per key; the Room Tag row still stat-scans the .fpdb tree on every call [P4] [subject:Architecture]
  - Details: SB3b review I2 residual (2026-10-29). `property_manager.py` mixed detection calls `other.get_properties()` inside the per-key loop, so `Room._tag_row` -> `annotation_choices.shipped_defs` / `library_defs` runs `_stamp` (one `_walk_fpdb` + `os.stat` per file, no reads) targets x keys times per panel build; the expensive `shipped_index` / `list_library` walks are cached by that stamp (24a2fdca). Fix shape: compute each target's properties once per build (hoist out of the key loop), or a build-start hook that the choice cache keys on. `property_manager.py`, `annotation_choices.py`, `room.py`.
- [ ] [type:bug] A pasted wall / gridline draws in the category colour, not its carried Graphic Override (colour / scale / opacity), until the next display pass [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: SB3b VC9 review M2 (2026-10-29). L283 makes paste carry `display_overrides`, and `paste_items` re-applies the carried Hide after `apply_category_defaults(wall)` (`model_space.py` paste wall branch) and `_register_gridline`; but `apply_category_defaults` -> `display_manager.apply_display_to_item` applies the CATEGORY colour/scale/opacity with no `_display_overrides` lookup. Repro: Graphic-Override a wall red, Ctrl+C / Ctrl+V -> the copy's dict holds the red override but it draws in the category colour until a DM pass or undo (`_apply_vals_to_item(..., respect_overrides=True)`). Fix shape: one `respect_overrides=True` apply in place of the category apply + re-hide. `model_space.py`, `display_manager.py`.
- [ ] [type:feature] Switching a block to Annotative rescales its contents to printed size (or offers to), plus a printed-size hint in the Block Editor [P1] [subject:UX]
  - Details: SB2a smoke, user, 2026-10-24: "if make it annotative - it disappears in plan view". The block didn't vanish. It was authored at model size (text 100 mm tall, about 755 mm from the origin), and Annotative reads authored mm as PRINTED mm. At 1:100 the text becomes 10 m tall and lands about 75 m from its insertion point; under Fixed size it lands thousands of px off-screen. Probe: identical at base `6e41d69b` and HEAD, border on or off, field text or plain (SB1c behaviour as designed, `annotative.annotative_factor`). Ask at Phase 2: does the Scale mode switch (Block Editor > Properties) rescale the contents by 1/drawing scale about the origin automatically, ask, or only warn? Is a real-size->annotative switch on an already-placed definition one undo step? What hint does the editor show (a printed-size readout on text height, a sheet-size ghost)? `block_properties_info.py`, `model_space.py` (`set_block_meta`), `annotative.py`, `block_editor.py`. ref: block-system SB1c scale modes.
- [ ] [type:bug] Explode one level of an annotative parent whose nested child is annotative double-scales the child [P2] [subject:Architecture]
  - Details: SB1c G-B (2026-10-23). Nested blocks are flattened at compile and take the parent's mode (user ruling, reconfirmed at SB1c smoke). One-level explode (`block_explode.py` `explode_instances`, nested child creation) bakes the size you see into the parent's loose geometry but recreates the nested child as a top-level annotative instance, which then re-applies its own factor (x drawing scale twice). Decide: bake the child to real size, or keep it annotative at authored scale. Guard: explode at 1:100 -> child extent unchanged on screen.
- [ ] [type:bug] SectionManager keeps its construction-time ScaleManager after project load / clear [P2] [subject:Architecture]
  - Details: SB1c VC9 fix round (2026-10-23). `scene_io.py` (~:214-216, ~:527) replaces `Model_Space.scale_manager` on load, but `SectionManager` (`section_manager.py` ~:81, used ~:198/:217/:300) keeps the one it was built with, so section ppm / format / drawing scale go stale after Open. SB1c's annotative path already reads `sc._ms.scale_manager` (commit 57a48824); every other `_sm` reader is still stale. Fix: read through the model each time or rebind on load. Guard via MainWindow open of a calibrated project.

- [ ] [type:design] SB8 — Linear (two-point) System blocks: placement type chosen in the block definition (one-click point blocks today vs two-click linear blocks), instance length exposed as an attribute [P2] [subject:Architecture]
  - Details: user, 2026-10-13. A linear block is placed by two clicks — the first sets the insertion point, the second sets direction + length via the HUD — and the instance stores that length as a per-placement driving dimension (from the "Instance parameters" design task, which keeps the driving-dimension mechanism). Attribute tie-in (SB1a): the stored length (and angle) are instance-sourced attributes, so block text can show `@[Length]`, and later host-sourced keys (SB2) can bind to them. Linear System block candidates: **model dimension annotation** (`DimensionAnnotation` in `annotations.py` — two-point + offset witness lines; see "Spec session: annotations"), **gridline** (line + leaders + bubble end blocks — "Gridline built from primitives"; revisits SB4b's "line stays bespoke" composite rule), **pipe** plan block ("Pipe as a linear Feature"). Open questions for the grill: what the Block Editor authors for a linear block (a stretch axis / which primitives stretch vs stay fixed, end-block slots shared with linetype end types LT6/D-L19), whether a dimension's offset is a third click or an attribute, and how re-editing length works (grips at both ends vs a property field). Depends SB1a (attributes), instance parameters (driving dims). `block_definition.py`, `block_instance.py`, `model_view.py` (placement), `annotations.py`, `gridline.py`.

## Block Editor constraint system (spec: `docs/specs/parametric-constraint-system.md`)

One constraint type per session, in order (spec §12). Every session: §11 guard tests (math / real-scene E2E drag / save-reopen / undo / diagnostics pixel-sampled / ribbon+icon) → full suite → user smoke + approval → flip the spec's §7.3/§12 row to built + stamp `verified-commit` → reconcile smoke deltas → only then the next session. Decisions D1–D20 + B1–B4 are ratified (2026-09-29 grill); do not re-litigate — a session pins only its own catalogue row (ref order, helper fields, degenerate cases) before building.

- [ ] [type:maint] Block Editor axis snaps -- remaining guard gaps from the axis-snap reviews [P4] [subject:Testing]
  - Details: axis-snap build reviews (2026-10-31), Minors filed not applied. Add guards in `tests/test_axis_snap.py`: (1) Offset of a semicircle `ArcItem` (0-180) centred on the X axis with MID / QUA snaps off is NOT collinear (`SnapEngine._collinear_axes`), so the X axis still snaps; (2) `snap_perpendicular` off drops the axis PER foot; (3) `from_point` on the axis omits PER; (4) a held axis result under `find(held=)` hysteresis; (5) dwell-on-origin then slide along X with NEA on resolves to the `x_axis` nearest (pins the outcome `test_origin_snap::test_align_dwell_on_origin_tracks_its_axes` now sidesteps); (6) the hot-axis cue under a real `move()` + `processEvents()` with no explicit repaint (today's test repaints the whole viewport). Spec: `snapping-engine.md` s4.2.
- [ ] [type:feature] Expose placed-block primitives to constraints in model space (plan scene), like the Block Editor's nested handles [P1] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: user ask 2026-10-30. The Block Editor already exposes every printed child primitive of a nested instance (`_NestedAdapter`, NB1-NB9, closed 2026-10-15 "Nested block primitives participate in constraints"), but the plan scene's constraint capability is `_plan_ref_types()` = (`WallSegment`, `GridlineItem`) only (`constraint_controller.py`), so a placed `BlockInstance` in model space offers no handles to pick mode, Smart Dimension or Auto Constrain. Widens the ratified plan scope (spec D2 "frozen at insert" as superseded by Editor/Build slice 2's `ref_types`) -- needs a grill + design (Large): which instance pose vars the plan solve may move (x y rot; mirror/scale fixed per NB?), block x wall/gridline relations, hosted/annotative blocks, Explode remap in plan, file-format handle names on plan instances, undo/save-reopen. Specs: `parametric-constraint-system.md` D2/D5/s5.1 + the NB design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-nested-block-constraint-handles-design.md`. Code: `constraint_controller.py`, `sketch_adapters.py`, `block_snap_geometry.py`.
  - **Phase 2 RATIFIED 2026-10-31 (grill Q1-Q9, user-approved definition; resume a fresh `/todo` at Phase 3 in worktree `.worktrees/plan-block-constraints`, branch `feat/plan-block-constraints`; do not re-ask):** End goal (Q1): lay out equipment -- tie placed blocks to walls / gridlines / each other (offset from a face, centred between gridlines, rows) and they follow when building geometry moves. AC1 (Q7): a plan `BlockInstance` offers every printed child handle + `<uid>:ins` to pick mode, Smart Dimension and selection-first adds (NB parity). AC2 (Q3): the solve moves x y rot only; mirror / scale fixed. AC3 (Q5): walls / gridlines never yield to a block edit -- a dragged / Moved block slides only where its constraints allow, a move needing building geometry to move is refused; moving a wall / gridline carries its blocks. AC4 (Q2): gridline-hosted blocks (`BlockInstance.host`) are reference-only -- handles offered, constrainable-to, never moved by a solve; they still ride their gridline. AC5 (Q4): on return from a definition edit, constraints whose handles still resolve re-solve as one undo step; ones whose handle is gone stay in the file, flagged in the constraints list, inert (same for a definition swap). AC6: save / reopen, undo / redo, Delete cascade carry block constraints. AC7 (Q8): Copy / Paste / Array keep the D30 rule (internal kept, outside ties dropped). AC8: hidden-level blocks solve, picks visible only (existing plan rule). Out of scope -> follow-ups at Phase 4: Auto Constrain on block placement (Q6), copies keeping outside ties (Q8), definition-flagged constraint points (Q7 C), plan Explode remap (plan Explode is refused). Q9: older builds drop block records on load -- accepted, no SAVE_VERSION bump. VC3 guards: corner->wall-face distance then a wall drag moves the block (RED with plan `ref_types` reverted); a block drag leaves the wall put and slides parallel; a hosted block never moves under a solve, still rides a gridline move; a definition edit moving a constrained child re-solves in one undo step, deleting it leaves the record flagged inert; save-reopen / undo-redo / Delete cascade; rotation solves, mirror / scale untouched. 1b reuse (re-verified): REUSE `_NestedAdapter` (no role checks; write = `set_block_pos` / `set_block_rotation`, same bake as Move `translate`), `posed_primitives`, `BlockInstance._uid` to_dict, plan `capture()` / `restore()` undo, `on_items_removed`, `PickState._candidates` via `items()`; GENERALIZE `_PLAN_PARTICIPATING` / `_plan_ref_types` + one plan-uid accessor shared by `scene_io._plan_uids` and `ConstraintController.primitive_uids` (`_plan_record_resolves`); GAP hosted reference-only, per-class "never yields" weighting (walls / gridlines fixed against block edits), definition-change re-solve + lost-handle flag (NB4 gap `todo_open.md` line on `on_definition_changed`), P4 probe: 3D / elevation / attribute refresh after a solve write.

- [ ] [type:maint] Batched point family for edge midpoints (`_mid`) if midpoint-heavy sketches lag [P4] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: CS3b review Minor (2026-10-12): `_mid` exprs carry no `fam`, so an axis row on a midpoint takes the per-row `fn` path in `_RowBatch` (correct, slower); line `points()` also costs ~3 µs more per call (`_mid` closure; measured). Commit path unaffected (0 `points()` calls on a reused build, probed). Only act with a D18-style bench showing a midpoint-heavy component over its bar. `sketch_adapters.py` `_mid`, `sketch_solver.py` `register_point_family`. +CS3c (2026-10-13 review M4): text box handles (`_text_point_fn`) also carry no `fam` -- batch them with the midpoints if a text-heavy sketch lags.

- [ ] [type:design] Pattern relation for Array copies (Linear / 2D / Polar) — rigid copies + spacing + angle [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: 2026-10-16 auto-constrain grill Q13 (user ruling: nothing in v1). `commit_array` already copies each copy's internal constraints (D30) but no spacing relation exists; faking one with two-point `dim_distance` records fixes a distance but not the copy's pose. Design the deferred `pattern` catalogue row (spec §12 "patterns"): a SolidWorks-style sketch pattern relation (instances rigid to the seed, spacing + angle / count driving), its file record, edit seams (Delete / Explode pattern), copy rule, and whether Array commits create it automatically under the Auto Constrain toggle. ref: parametric-constraint-system §7.3 / §8 Array row; `modify_tools_controller.commit_array`, `_array_transforms`.
- [ ] [type:design] Auto Constrain on grip-drag release and the Move tool's destination click [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: 2026-10-16 auto-constrain grill Q14 (user ruling: placement + Offset only in v1). SolidWorks infers a Coincident when an endpoint is dropped on another point. Decide: a grip released on a point / edge snap → `coincident` / `point_on_curve` (same Shift rule); a Move whose base point was a handle and whose destination snaps → join; interaction with D46 partner-watch (a moved partner is excluded as a snap target) and D47 grip rules. ref: `GripHandle` release seam, `Model_Space.move_items`, AC1's resolver.
- [ ] [type:feature] Auto Constrain: intersection, tangent and quadrant snaps [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: 2026-10-16 auto-constrain grill Q5 (out of v1). INT → two `point_on_curve` records (the point on both segments / curves; redundancy risk when they already meet there — AC1 clean-only applies); TAN → `point_on_curve` + `tangent` between the new line and the circle / arc; QUA → `coincident` on a quadrant handle — depends on "Circle quadrant points (0 / 90 / 180 / 270 deg) as constraint handles" (this section), until then no record. Depends AC1.
- [ ] [type:feature] Offset gap constraints for rect / polygon / polyline / ellipse offsets [P4] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: 2026-10-16 auto-constrain grill Q12 (lines + circles / arcs only in v1, AC4). A rect / polygon offset has no parallel-shape relation (four per-side point-line dims would over-capture); a polyline offset's per-segment Parallel + gap dims are redundant at mitered vertices; an ellipse offset is not a true offset curve. Needs its own design pass (possibly an `offset` relation type). Depends AC4.
  - Note 2026-10-17: AC4 met -- merged to main `1adf8dae` (0.53.0). Extend `auto_constrain.plan_offset` (spec §13 D155; it returns `[]` for these shapes today) -- the gate and the one-undo `auto_join` wiring in `ModifyToolsController._auto_constrain_offset` (D156) already cover every shape.
- [ ] [type:design] Smart Dimensions on the plan canvas — dimension / edit loose 2D primitives in model space, not only in the Block Editor [P2] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: user, 2026-10-13. Prerequisite for "Retire the editable selection dimension readouts". Today the Smart Dimension button is Block Editor-only (`main.py` `_editor_only(b_d, …)`) and the constraint system runs on the editor scene. Decide whether plan-canvas dims are driving (constraints on model geometry: solver scope, undo, serialization in the `.fpd`) or annotation-only dims that edit the primitive directly, and what replaces the readout's one-field HUD edit. ref: parametric-constraint-system, 2d-geometry (readouts section).
- [ ] [type:feature] Select / edit existing dims while the Smart Dimension tool is live (CS4 review F9) [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: D50 keeps the tool live, but label pick / double-click edit are gated on select mode (`Model_View._glyph_ctl`), so a dim can't be selected or re-edited without Esc first. (Re-worded 2026-10-14: since CS4b D63 the first click on an edge no longer opens the HUD -- the placement click does -- so the old fast-double-click clause is moot.) SolidWorks lets you double-click a dim inside the tool. Found by the CS4 whole-diff review. (Note 2026-10-14: since CS5 D82 every dim is drawn while the tool is live, but it still can't be selected or edited there.)
- [ ] [type:feature] Dimension terminator picker in Settings (choose the End Types end Smart Dimensions draw) [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: CS4b D61 / Q16 (user 2026-10-14): the terminator is the End Types end named by QSettings `dimension/terminator` (default `Arrow`) with no UI. Add a picker (System Settings, listing the `capabilities.end_choices` folder ends) and repaint open editors on change -- also put the terminator in `constraint_paint._frame_key` (today a changed setting / rewritten end file shows only after the next pan / zoom / commit). `dim_graphics.terminator_name` / `terminator_def`.
- [ ] [type:feature] Slide a dim's text along the dim line / outside the witness lines [P3] [subject:UX]
  - Details: CS4b Q9 follow-up (user 2026-10-14): a placed dim's drag (D64) moves only its perpendicular offset; the text stays centred (or past the finish terminator when short, D66). SolidWorks lets the text slide along / outside. Needs a second `label` field (e.g. `along`) -- spec D62 + `dim_graphics.layout_dim`.
- [ ] [type:bug] Two-point dims on a rect / polygon's own handle pair keep the old handle names through Flip / Mirror [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: CS4b final review (2026-10-14, probed): rect (0,0)-(300,100), dim `tl`-`br` offset -40, Flip across y=200 -- `RectangleItem.manip_reflect` renormalises, so `tl`/`br` name the other diagonal; the carried anchor (D65) is then measured against that line (offset +32): the dim neither measures the mirrored diagonal nor sits at its mirrored place (same length, so the solve is unaffected). `_refit_edge` covers single-edge dims only; extend it to point pairs on one item (remap each handle to the one nearest its mirrored point), or caveat D65.
- [ ] [type:bug] Smart Dimension interaction polish: bare D mid-placement restarts; no preview until the first move; HUD anchor while dragging; wide terminator corners don't pick [P4] [subject:UX]
  - Details: CS4b reviews (2026-10-14). (1) Bare D while a placement is pending rebuilds the PickState (pending dropped) where the lit ribbon button would un-toggle -- D63 says D = the button. (2) A selected line / D skips to placement but `PickState.cursor` is None until the first mouse move, so no preview shows at once (seed it from `QCursor.pos()`). (3) A press on a dim whose HUD is open arms a drag; the HUD stays at the old label spot. (4) `dim_graphics.hit_dim` picks terminators only through the dim-line / stub segments, so a user end wider than the 6 px tolerance has corners that don't pick (D64).
- [ ] [type:maint] Unify the two `_seg_dist` helpers (`dim_graphics` + `constraint_controller`) [P4] [subject:Architecture]
  - Details: CS4b final review (2026-10-14): identical point-to-segment math in `firepro3d/dim_graphics.py` and `firepro3d/constraint_controller.py`; the controller can import the `dim_graphics` one (no cycle). A tolerance tweak in one would silently diverge pick-marker hover from dim-line pick.
- [ ] [type:maint] Draw Smart Dimension witness / dim lines through `crisp_stroke.stroke` instead of `dim_graphics._crisp_seg` [P4] [subject:Architecture]
  - Details: CS4b lessons (2026-10-14): a pixel guard showed 1 px dim lines half-toned (`#69558e` vs `#b48cff`); `dim_graphics` re-implemented axis-aligned pixel-centre snapping (`_crisp_seg` / `_crisp_tip`, DPR-aware) while `firepro3d/crisp_stroke.py` (`stroke(painter, path, pen)`, MW 2026-10-07) already draws exactly-axis runs crisply -- the 1b reuse sweep missed it. Fold onto `crisp_stroke` (keep the terminator tip aligned with the stroked line); parity = `tests/test_constraint_dims.py` pixel guards + `test_dim_graphics.py`.
- [ ] [type:maint] parametric-constraint-system spec wording: D62 auto side for a rect side; the dim colour order is restated in D48 / D60 / section 10 (Rule A) [P4] [subject:Documentation]
  - Details: CS4b T1 spec review (2026-10-14). (1) D62 says 'away from the scene-bounds centre of its edge's item' -- say explicitly that a rect / polygon side uses the shape's centre and that 'screen-left' applies to any near-vertical dim. (2) Pre-existing: the dim state colour order (selected > hover > red > suppressed > Reference > redundant > dimension) is restated in D48, D60 and section 10 Canvas -- keep it in one row and link.
- [ ] [type:design] A stale whole-item Fix should show, not just sit out silently [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: CS8 review (2026-10-15). A whole-item Fix whose `helper.v0` length no longer matches its item's variable count (a polyline vertex added / removed in-session) is skipped by `ConstraintController.active()` and pins nothing (`build_fix` guard), but it is neither red nor inert, so its glyph / panel row still look live. Decide: mark it inert (invalid) at the edit that changed the count, re-capture `v0` on that edit, or show it red. `constraint_controller.py` (`active`, `_fix_helper_ok`), `sketch_solver.py` (`build_fix`). ref: parametric-constraint-system FP2p.
- [ ] [type:maint] Decompose `constraint_controller.py` — PickState, panel / adapter, copy rules out [P3] [subject:Architecture]
  - Details: past the 1,000-line tripwire. Natural seams (CS4 reuse sweep): `PickState` → its own module (dims added a flow branch), panel text / rows + `ConstraintAdapter` → `constraint_panel.py`, D30/D44/D54 copy rules → `sketch_model`. Pure relocation: parity = the constraint test set. Note 2026-10-14: CS4b added the dim placement flow (`dim_precheck` / `_place_dim` / `begin_dim_placement` / `reset_dim_pick`), the drag API (`begin_dim_drag` ... `cancel_dim_drag`) and the D65 carry (`_dim_anchors` / `_refit_edge` / `_carry_dim_labels`) -- a natural dim-flow module seam.
- [ ] [type:bug] Block-instance Flip can leave an origin -> nested-edge point-line dim unsatisfied [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: CS15 review (2026-10-16), unverified by probe. D137: an in-place Flip of plain geometry refuses an origin -> line dim whose mirror misses the origin (the solve honours the edited vars and rolls back), but a block instance's mirror / rotation live outside the solver variables (D45), so a partly free instance carrying an origin -> `<path>:h` edge dim may flip with the dim silently violated. Same gap for any ground-ref dim on an instance. Repro first; then refuse at `flip_refusal_targets` / `refuse_grounded` or re-check after the flip. `constraint_controller.py` (`_flip_dim_signs`, `refuse_grounded`), `modify_tools_controller.py`. ref: parametric-constraint-system D76 / D137 / D45.
- [ ] [type:feature] Circle quadrant points (0 / 90 / 180 / 270 deg) as constraint handles [P2] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: user, 2026-10-15 (nested-block-handles smoke). A loose circle exposes only `center` + `curve` (spec 5.3: grips 1-4 are radius grips, "no handle in v1"); expose four derived quadrant points `q0 q90 q180 q270` = c + r*(cos a, -sin a) over (cx, cy, r) on `_CircleAdapter` (nested circles then inherit them through `_nested_table`, NB2). Decide: also arcs (quadrants inside the span only)? grip map 1-4 -> q*? `sketch_adapters.py`, spec 5.1 circle row + 5.3.
- [ ] [type:feature] Snap: nested blocks' insertion points (grandchild `ins`) as snap points -- parity with constraints [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: nested-block-handles build (2026-10-15, NB2/NB3): constraints expose a nested record's composed origin (`<rec uid>:ins`) through `block_snap_geometry.posed_primitives(paths=True)` origins, but SNAP still has no nested insertion point (closed "Snap/ALIGN completeness for blocks" note). Consume the same `origins` in `snap_engine` / `BlockInstance.snap_primitives`. `snapping-engine.md`.
- [ ] [type:feature] Parent Block Editor re-solves when a child definition changes in another tab [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: nested-block-handles design NB4 known gap (2026-10-15): a child primitive moved / deleted in its own tab (commit) leaves the parent canvas visibly unsatisfied (or its row Unsupported) until the parent's next edit; hooking `BlockInstance.on_definition_changed` -> `constraint_ctl` re-solve crosses tab undo stacks (a parent undo step for a child edit?). Decide the undo semantics first. `constraint_controller.py`, `block_instance.py`, `block_definition.py` (`users_of` / `invalidate`).
- [ ] [type:maint] Legacy block definitions without primitive `uid`s key nested constraint handles positionally [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: nested-block-handles build (NB3, 2026-10-15): `block_explode.record_key` falls back to `"#<index>"` for a record with no `uid`, so a parent constraint on such a child handle silently re-points after a child insert / delete (seen in the T6 guard, which gives its fixture uids). The spec's fix is that a legacy definition gains uids on its next save (6.1) -- consider minting them on load / first table build instead. `block_definition.py`, `block_snap_geometry.py`.
- [ ] [type:design] Smart Dimension between parallel edges — hold both parallel, act about both, elegant witness lines [P2] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: CS5 smoke (user, 2026-10-14). CS5 D81 dims two parallel edges as `dim_point_line` (2nd edge's midpoint → 1st edge's line): it does not keep them parallel and acts about one line's centre point. Wanted: the linear dim maintains parallelism between the two reference edges (not a true Parallel constraint, CS9), acts on both, and draws witness lines elegantly for offset / partly-overlapping segments. Separate design session (user). Meanwhile D81 ships as built (user ruling 2026-10-14). ref: parametric-constraint-system D81, §7.3 point-line row; `constraint_dims.classify_dim` / `_parallel_refs`, `constraint_controller._pl_apply`.
- [ ] [type:bug] Selected circumscribed polygon draws its circumcircle as the reference circle instead of its defining incircle [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: CS5 smoke (user, 2026-10-14): "both circumscribed and inscribed show an inscribed reference circle". `RegularPolygonItem.paint` (geometry_2d.py, the `isSelected()` dashed reference circle) draws at `_circumradius()` for both modes; for a circumscribed polygon the defining circle is the incircle (R = apothem) -- placement (`geometry_drawing_controller` `_polygon_ref_circle`) already draws the defining radius, so selection and placement disagree. VC7-proved pre-existing: a recording-painter probe at base `02955ab1` and at the CS5 branch both draw radius 69.28 for a circumscribed hexagon with R = 60 (inscribed: 60). Fix: draw `self._radius_mm` (the defining circle). ref: 2d-geometry polygon reference circle (c43771c1 / c4b58f83).
- [ ] [type:feature] Splines in the constraint system — endpoints first (Coincident / H / V on spline end points), point-on-spline later [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: user question at the CS3 smoke (2026-10-03). Splines are excluded by D5 (no adapter, no handles, no pick markers). First cut: a spline adapter exposing its two end points (derived from control points; fit vs control-point splines differ — decide in the grill) so Coincident joins a spline to other geometry; point-on-spline needs a curve residual + projection. Spec §5.1/D5 amendment. `sketch_adapters.py`, `constraint_controller.py`.

The padlock/`AlignmentConstraint` half is retired by CS1; if this runs first, retire both. Whole-repo grep (`"align"` mode, `_press_align`, `_execute_align`, Shift+L shortcut, `tests/test_align_tool.py`, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-04-30-align-tool-design.md`, `scene-tools.md`). Confirm with the user that nothing else rides on the tool before removal.
- [ ] [type:bug] Explode drops a closed polyline's closing segment [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: found reading code in the 2026-09-29 constraint grounding (NOT reproduced yet — run the repro first): `SceneTools.explode_selected_items` loops `range(len(pts)-1)` and ignores `_closed`, so a closed N-vertex polyline explodes to N−1 lines. `scene_tools.py`. ref: scene-tools.md.

- [ ] [type:maint] D18 rect-heavy D35 body-drag frame ~10-13 ms vs the 8 ms bar -- sparse/banded solve of A = J W^-1 J^T [P2] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: the one open D18 case after the 2026-10-03 perf task (user ruling: ship, keep the bench strict-xfail -- `tests/test_constraint_live_drag.py::test_d18_rect_heavy_body_drag_frame`). Floor per frame on the 100-rect + 100-line one-component case: ~4 dense 299x299 LU solves (both D34 passes, ~1 ms each single-threaded on this host) + Qt writes of the ~193 items that really move (~3.5 ms). First step = an isolated A/B bench on the real A: a banded solve (rows ordered along the chain) vs `np.linalg.solve`; scipy `splu` would reopen B1 (spec says no scipy; note `requirements.txt` lists `scipy>=1.14` but the venv lacks it -- reconcile either way). Measured dead ends: warm-starting the plain D34 pass from the translate result needs the same 3 evaluations. Also open: arc start/end and polygon-vertex rows still evaluate per row (only raw/const/rect are batched families). `sketch_solver.py`, `constraint_controller.py`. ref: parametric-constraint-system.md §9 / D18.
- [ ] [type:maint] Plan wall-chain drag frame ~2.2 s at 200 walls (Horizontal + Coincident chain, one wall-end grip frame) -- profile `ConstraintController.drag` / `_solve` at plan scale before choosing a fix [P2] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: found 2026-10-31 by plan-block-constraints Task 10 (PB3 bench). Interleaved fresh-process medians of 31 frames: branch base e51c9627 2304 / 2256 ms, HEAD 2304 / 2180 ms -- pre-existing, not the two-tier refactor (tied blocks add ~1 %, guarded by `tests/test_plan_block_constraints.py::test_pb_wall_chain_tied_blocks_overhead_ratio`). Not root-caused: ~800 unknowns is far beyond a dense-LU cost (cf. the D18 sparse/banded line above), so measure where the time goes (solver iterations, Python per-row loops, Qt writes) first. Scene recipe = that test's `chain()` helper.

- [ ] [type:feature] Horizontal / Vertical on an exactly perpendicular line rotates it instead of reporting a conflict [P2] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: CS2 (2026-10-02, D36 consequence). A line has no rotation variable, so least-change H on an exactly vertical line (or V on an exactly horizontal one) can only collapse it to a point -> D36 conflict -> red. Pre-CS2 it silently collapsed. SolidWorks rotates the line about its midpoint, keeping its length. Needs a design pass: a length-preserving goal (or a temporary rotation parameterisation) for the add-time solve only; keep D22/D34 for everything else. ref: parametric-constraint-system.md D22/D36. +1 occurrence (CS10, 2026-10-15): the length-preserving add-time solve now EXISTS for Parallel / Perpendicular -- `ConstraintController._rotate_first` (`ROTATE_FIRST`, temporary `dim_distance` holds through `_solve(hold=)`, plain fallback; PP5/PP6). H / V can likely join `ROTATE_FIRST` (the hold rows also cover a tilted -- not only exactly perpendicular -- line that H / V today shrinks); still decide whether H / V should rotate (user ruling).
- [ ] [type:maint] Diagnostics attribution consistency — conflicts in fix-list order, redundancy equalities-before-rows [P3] [subject:CAD]
  - Details: CS2 reviews (2026-10-02, minor): `Diagnostics.conflicts` is attributed in fix-list order, not `cid_rank` (unused for red — red is admission, D38 — but misleading); redundancy marks a later row amber when a later EQUALITY made two earlier rows identical (documented in §7.4). Also `_equality_redundancy` sorts cids missing from `cid_rank` aliases-before-fixes. Unify on `cid_rank` across classes if it ever matters. `sketch_solver.py`.

Either simplify load() to one solve or add a guard that needs per-group writes. `constraint_controller.py`.
